Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The search operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 213. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 325 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on search structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 213. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 325. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The search variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 785 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 896 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to search theory, we decompose the problem into 785 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 896. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
